\displaystyle+T\left[(\bm{\Sigma}^{\xi})^{-1}\bm{\Lambda}\bm{P}^{-1}\bm{\Lambda}^{\prime}(\bm{\Sigma}^{\xi})^{-1}\bm{\Lambda}\widehat{\bm{\Gamma}}^{F\xi}(\bm{\Sigma}^{\xi})^{-1}\bm{\Lambda}\bm{P}^{-1}\otimes\bm{P}^{-1}\bm{\Lambda}^{\prime}(\bm{\Sigma}^{\xi})^{-1}\right]\bm{C}_{n,r}\hskip 9.24994pt\text{\it A.7.3}

\displaystyle+T\left[(\bm{\Sigma}^{\xi})^{-1}\bm{\Lambda}\bm{P}^{-1}\bm{\Lambda}^{\prime}(\bm{\Sigma}^{\xi})^{-1}\widehat{\bm{\Gamma}}^{\xi F}\bm{\Lambda}^{\prime}(\bm{\Sigma}^{\xi})^{-1}\bm{\Lambda}\bm{P}^{-1}\otimes\bm{P}^{-1}\bm{\Lambda}^{\prime}(\bm{\Sigma}^{\xi})^{-1}\right]\bm{C}_{n,r}\hskip 9.24994pt\text{\it A.7.4}

\displaystyle+T\left[(\bm{\Sigma}^{\xi})^{-1}\bm{\Lambda}\bm{P}^{-1}\otimes\bm{P}^{-1}\bm{\Lambda}^{\prime}(\bm{\Sigma}^{\xi})^{-1}\bm{\Lambda}\bm{\Lambda}^{\prime}(\bm{\Sigma}^{\xi})^{-1}\bm{\Lambda}\bm{P}^{-1}\bm{\Lambda}^{\prime}(\bm{\Sigma}^{\xi})^{-1}\right]\bm{C}_{n,r}\hskip 9.24994pt\text{\it A.8.1}

\displaystyle+T\left[(\bm{\Sigma}^{\xi})^{-1}\bm{\Lambda}\bm{P}^{-1}\otimes\bm{P}^{-1}\bm{\Lambda}^{\prime}(\bm{\Sigma}^{\xi})^{-1}\widehat{\bm{\Gamma}}^{\xi}(\bm{\Sigma}^{\xi})^{-1}\bm{\Lambda}\bm{P}^{-1}\bm{\Lambda}^{\prime}(\bm{\Sigma}^{\xi})^{-1}\right]\bm{C}_{n,r}\hskip 9.24994pt\text{\it A.8.2}

\displaystyle+T\left[(\bm{\Sigma}^{\xi})^{-1}\bm{\Lambda}\bm{P}^{-1}\otimes\bm{P}^{-1}\bm{\Lambda}^{\prime}(\bm{\Sigma}^{\xi})^{-1}\bm{\Lambda}\widehat{\bm{\Gamma}}^{F\xi}(\bm{\Sigma}^{\xi})^{-1}\bm{\Lambda}\bm{P}^{-1}\bm{\Lambda}^{\prime}(\bm{\Sigma}^{\xi})^{-1}\right]\bm{C}_{n,r}\hskip 9.24994pt\text{\it A.8.3}

\displaystyle+T\left[(\bm{\Sigma}^{\xi})^{-1}\bm{\Lambda}\bm{P}^{-1}\otimes\bm{P}^{-1}\bm{\Lambda}^{\prime}(\bm{\Sigma}^{\xi})^{-1}\widehat{\bm{\Gamma}}^{\xi F}\bm{\Lambda}^{\prime}(\bm{\Sigma}^{\xi})^{-1}\bm{\Lambda}\bm{P}^{-1}\bm{\Lambda}^{\prime}(\bm{\Sigma}^{\xi})^{-1}\right]\bm{C}_{n,r}\hskip 9.24994pt\text{\it A.8.4}

\displaystyle-T\left[(\bm{\Sigma}^{\xi})^{-1}\otimes\bm{P}^{-1}\right]\hskip 9.24994pt\text{\it B.1}

\displaystyle+T\left[(\bm{\Sigma}^{\xi})^{-1}\bm{\Lambda}\bm{\Lambda}^{\prime}(\bm{\Sigma}^{\xi})^{-1}\otimes\bm{P}^{-1}\right]\hskip 9.24994pt\text{\it B.2.1}

\displaystyle+T\left[(\bm{\Sigma}^{\xi})^{-1}\widehat{\bm{\Gamma}}^{\xi F}\bm{\Lambda}^{\prime}(\bm{\Sigma}^{\xi})^{-1}\otimes\bm{P}^{-1}\right]\hskip 9.24994pt\text{\it B.2.4}

\displaystyle[\ldots]

\displaystyle[\ldots]

\displaystyle-T\left[(\bm{\Sigma}^{\xi})^{-1}\bm{\Lambda}\bm{P}^{-1}\bm{\Lambda}^{\prime}(\bm{\Sigma}^{\xi})^{-1}\bm{\Lambda}\bm{\Lambda}^{\prime}(\bm{\Sigma}^{\xi})^{-1}\otimes\bm{P}^{-1}\right]\hskip 9.24994pt\text{\it B.3.1}

\displaystyle-T\left[(\bm{\Sigma}^{\xi})^{-1}\bm{\Lambda}\bm{P}^{-1}\bm{\Lambda}^{\prime}(\bm{\Sigma}^{\xi})^{-1}\widehat{\bm{\Gamma}}^{\xi}(\bm{\Sigma}^{\xi})^{-1}\otimes\bm{P}^{-1}\right]\hskip 9.24994pt\text{\it B.3.2}

\displaystyle-T\left[(\bm{\Sigma}^{\xi})^{-1}\bm{\Lambda}\bm{P}^{-1}\bm{\Lambda}^{\prime}(\bm{\Sigma}^{\xi})^{-1}\bm{\Lambda}\widehat{\bm{\Gamma}}^{F\xi}(\bm{\Sigma}^{\xi})^{-1}\otimes\bm{P}^{-1}\right]\hskip 9.24994pt\text{\it B.3.3}

\displaystyle-T\left[(\bm{\Sigma}^{\xi})^{-1}\bm{\Lambda}\bm{P}^{-1}\bm{\Lambda}^{\prime}(\bm{\Sigma}^{\xi})^{-1}\widehat{\bm{\Gamma}}^{\xi F}\bm{\Lambda}^{\prime}(\bm{\Sigma}^{\xi})^{-1}\otimes\bm{P}^{-1}\right]\hskip 9.24994pt\text{\it B.3.4}

(A.83)

Hence,
\|\bm{h}_{ii}(\bm{\mathcal{X}};\bm{\varphi})\|=O_{\mathrm{P}}(T)

\displaystyle\frac{1}{T}

\displaystyle+\frac{M_{\Lambda}^{2}}{C_{\xi}^{2}}\left\|\left\{\bm{\Lambda}^{\prime}(\bm{\Sigma}^{\xi})^{-1}\bm{\Lambda}\right\}^{-1}\right\|^{2}\Bigg\{1+2\left\|\frac{1}{T}\bm{\Lambda}^{\prime}(\bm{\Sigma}^{\xi})^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}{\bm{\xi}}_{t}\mathbf{F}_{t}^{\prime}\right\|\Bigg\}

\displaystyle+\frac{2M_{\Lambda}}{C_{\xi}^{2}}\left\|\left\{\bm{\Lambda}^{\prime}(\bm{\Sigma}^{\xi})^{-1}\bm{\Lambda}\right\}^{-1}\right\|\,\left\|\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{F}_{t}{\xi}_{it}\right\|+\frac{M_{\Lambda}^{2}}{C_{\xi}^{2}}\left\|\left\{\bm{\Lambda}^{\prime}(\bm{\Sigma}^{\xi})^{-1}\bm{\Lambda}\right\}^{-1}\right\|^{2}\Bigg\{1+2\left\|\frac{1}{T}\bm{\Lambda}^{\prime}(\bm{\Sigma}^{\xi})^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}{\bm{\xi}}_{t}\mathbf{F}_{t}^{\prime}\right\|\Bigg\}

\displaystyle+O\left(\frac{1}{n^{2}}\right)+O_{\mathrm{P}}\left(\frac{1}{n^{2}\sqrt{T}}\right)+O\left(\frac{1}{n^{4}}\right)

\displaystyle=

(A.84)

Finally, from (
A.67
) we have

\displaystyle\mathrm{d}\bm{S}^{\prime}(\bm{\mathcal{X}}|\bm{\mathcal{F}};\underline{\bm{\varphi}})

\displaystyle=\mathrm{d}\left(\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{F}_{t}(\mathbf{x}_{t}-\underline{\bm{\Lambda}}\mathbf{F}_{t})^{\prime}(\underline{\bm{\Sigma}}^{\xi})^{-1}\right)=-\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{F}_{t}^{\prime}\left(\mathrm{d}\underline{\bm{\Lambda}}^{\prime}\right)(\underline{\bm{\Sigma}}^{\xi})^{-1},

so

\text{vec}(\mathrm{d}\bm{S}^{\prime}(\bm{\mathcal{X}}|\bm{\mathcal{F}};\underline{\bm{\varphi}}))=-(\underline{\bm{\Sigma}}^{\xi})^{-1}\otimes\left(\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{F}_{t}^{\prime}\right)\text{vec}(\mathrm{d}\underline{\bm{\Lambda}}^{\prime}),

and

\bm{H}(\bm{\mathcal{X}}|\bm{\mathcal{F}};\underline{\bm{\varphi}})=-(\underline{\bm{\Sigma}}^{\xi})^{-1}\otimes\left(\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{F}_{t}^{\prime}\right)=-T(\underline{\bm{\Sigma}}^{\xi})^{-1}\otimes\mathbf{I}_{r},

(A.85)

because of Assumption
5
(b). And (
A.85
) computed in the true value of the parameters is

\bm{H}(\bm{\mathcal{X}}|\bm{\mathcal{F}};{\bm{\varphi}})=-T({\bm{\Sigma}}^{\xi})^{-1}\otimes\mathbf{I}_{r}.

(A.86)

From (

(A.87)

Hence,
\|\bm{h}_{ii}(\bm{\mathcal{X}}|\bm{\mathcal{F}};{\bm{\varphi}})\|=O_{\mathrm{P}}(T)
, and, by using (
A.87

\frac{1}{T}\left\|\bm{h}_{ii}(\bm{\mathcal{X}};\bm{\varphi})-\bm{h}_{ii}(\bm{\mathcal{X}}|\bm{\mathcal{F}};{\bm{\varphi}})\right\|=O_{\mathrm{P}}\left(\max\left(\frac{1}{n},\frac{1}{\sqrt{nT}}\right)\right),

which proves part (b).

For part (c) we provide two different equivalent proofs. First,

\displaystyle\left\|\bm{s}_{i}(\bm{\mathcal{X}};\widehat{\bm{\varphi}}^{\text{\tiny QML,E}})-\bm{s}_{i}(\bm{\mathcal{X}}|\bm{\mathcal{F}};\widehat{\bm{\varphi}}^{\text{\tiny QML,E}})\right\|\leq

\displaystyle\,\left\|\bm{s}_{i}(\bm{\mathcal{X}};{\bm{\varphi}})-\bm{s}_{i}(\bm{\mathcal{X}}|\bm{\mathcal{F}};{\bm{\varphi}})\right\|

\displaystyle+\left\|\bm{h}_{ii}(\bm{\mathcal{X}};{\bm{\varphi}})-\bm{h}_{ii}(\bm{\mathcal{X}}|\bm{\mathcal{F}};{\bm{\varphi}})\right\|\,\left\|\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{\text{\tiny QML,E}}-{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}\right\|+O_{\mathrm{P}}\left(\left\|\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{\text{\tiny QML,E}}-{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}\right\|^{2}\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\,O_{\mathrm{P}}\left(\max\left(\frac{T}{n},\frac{\sqrt{T}}{\sqrt{n}}\right)\right)+O_{\mathrm{P}}\left(\max\left(\frac{T}{n},\frac{\sqrt{T}}{\sqrt{n}}\right)\right)O_{\mathrm{P}}\left(\max\left(\frac{1}{n},\frac{1}{\sqrt{T}}\right)\right).

because of parts (a) and (b), and (
A.4
), which follows from Theorem
3
, which, in turn, holds even if we use the simpler log-likelihood (
13
) in place of the log-likelihood (
12
).
As a consequence,

\frac{1}{\sqrt{T}}\left\|\bm{s}_{i}(\bm{\mathcal{X}};\widehat{\bm{\varphi}}^{\text{\tiny QML,E}})-\bm{s}_{i}(\bm{\mathcal{X}}|\bm{\mathcal{F}};\widehat{\bm{\varphi}}^{\text{\tiny QML,E}})\right\|=O_{\mathrm{P}}\left(\max\left(\frac{\sqrt{T}}{n},\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\right)\right).

Alternatively, let
\widehat{\bm{\varphi}}^{\text{\tiny OLS}}=(\mathrm{vec}(\bm{\Lambda}^{\text{\tiny OLS}})^{\prime},\mathrm{vech}(\underline{\bm{\Gamma}}^{\xi})^{\prime})^{\prime}
which is the vector of parameters made of the OLS estimator of the loadings and any generic value of the idiosyncratic covariance matrix satisfying Assumption
2
. Then, notice that
\bm{s}_{i}(\bm{\mathcal{X}};\widehat{\bm{\varphi}}^{\text{\tiny QML,E}})=\mathbf{0}_{r}
by definition of QML estimator, and
\bm{s}_{i}(\bm{\mathcal{X}}|\bm{\mathcal{F}};\widehat{\bm{\varphi}}^{\text{\tiny OLS}})=\mathbf{0}_{r}
because the OLS estimator is the QML estimator when maximizing the conditional log-likelihood, and the OLS estimator of the loadings does not depend on the estimator of the idiosyncratic covariance. Recall also that

\left\|\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{\text{\tiny QML,E}}-{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{\text{\tiny OLS}}\right\|\leq\left\|\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{\text{\tiny QML,E}}-\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}\right\|+\left\|\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}-{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{\text{\tiny OLS}}\right\|=O_{\mathrm{P}}\left(\max\left(\frac{1}{n},\frac{1}{\sqrt{nT}}\right)\right),

because of Theorem
3
and (
A.4
) which hold even if we use the log-likelihood (
13
) in place of the log-likelihood (
12
). Moreover, from (
A.6
) it is straightforward to see that

\|\bm{h}_{ii}(\bm{\mathcal{X}}|\bm{\mathcal{F}};\underline{\bm{\varphi}})\|=O_{\mathrm{P}}(T)
, for any
\underline{\bm{\varphi}}\in\mathcal{O}_{n}
. Therefore,

\displaystyle\left\|\bm{s}_{i}(\bm{\mathcal{X}};\widehat{\bm{\varphi}}^{\text{\tiny QML,E}})-\bm{s}_{i}(\bm{\mathcal{X}}|\bm{\mathcal{F}};\widehat{\bm{\varphi}}^{\text{\tiny QML,E}})\right\|=

\displaystyle\,\left\|\bm{s}_{i}(\bm{\mathcal{X}}|\bm{\mathcal{F}};\widehat{\bm{\varphi}}^{\text{\tiny QML,E}})\right\|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\,\left\|\bm{s}_{i}(\bm{\mathcal{X}}|\bm{\mathcal{F}};\widehat{\bm{\varphi}}^{\text{\tiny OLS}})\right\|+\left\|\bm{h}_{ii}(\bm{\mathcal{X}}|\bm{\mathcal{F}};\widehat{\bm{\varphi}}^{\text{\tiny OLS}})\right\|\,\left\|\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{\text{\tiny QML,E}}-{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{\text{\tiny OLS}}\right\|

\displaystyle+O_{\mathrm{P}}\left(\left\|\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{\text{\tiny QML,E}}-{\bm{\lambda}}_{i}^{\text{\tiny OLS}}\right\|^{2}\right)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\,O_{\mathrm{P}}\left(T\right)O_{\mathrm{P}}\left(\max\left(\frac{1}{n},\frac{1}{\sqrt{nT}}\right)\right)=O_{\mathrm{P}}\left(\max\left(\frac{T}{n},\frac{\sqrt{T}}{\sqrt{n}}\right)\right).

This proves part (c).

For part (d), for any specific value of the parameters, say
\widetilde{\bm{\varphi}}
, define the
n^{2}r^{2}\times nr
matrices of third derivatives

with components

which are
r^{2}\times r
matrices. Then, from (
A.6

i
